[X11] Make IdleQueryX11 asynchronous via XSync IDLETIME alarms

About 30% of browser UI-thread hangs in
x11::Connection::WaitForResponse on Linux come from
ui::IdleQueryX11::IdleTime issuing a synchronous ScreenSaver::QueryInfo
request when X11ScreenOzone::CalculateIdleTime is called (most commonly
from IdlePollingService::OnLockStateChanged and PollIdleState while the
X server is in DPMS, screen lock, or a VT switch).

Rework IdleQueryX11 into a persistent event observer owned by
X11ScreenOzone that tracks user inactivity via XSync IDLETIME
PositiveComparison (>= 1000 ms) and NegativeComparison (<= 999 ms)
alarms, matching Wayland's ExtIdleNotifier and OrgKdeKwinIdle approach
with 1-second resolution and zero round trips per CalculateIdleTime
call. Retain a throttled asynchronous ScreenSaver::QueryInfo fallback
for X servers that do not expose an IDLETIME counter.

[iOS][Composebox] Remove kComposeboxServerSideState feature flag

kComposeboxServerSideState has launched on both iPhone and iPad, and all
EarlGrey test suites now run against server-driven state. Remove the
feature flag and legacy local eligibility fallbacks so attachment
options and tool availability rely solely on SearchboxConfig and
contextual search InputState.

- Delete kComposeboxServerSideState, its about_flags and
  flag-metadata.json entries, and EnableComposeboxServerSideState().
- Inline the server-state checks in ComposeboxInputStateManager via a
  shared InputTypeForAttachmentOption helper and delete the dead
  is*Locally fallback methods.
- Remove local-fallback unit test cases and configure SearchboxConfig
  rule sets directly in ComposeboxInputPlateMediatorTest.

[ozone/wayland] Handle wl_buffer.release for syncobj buffers

When the compositor supports linux-drm-syncobj, dmabuf buffers use the
kSyncobj sync method, and their wl_buffer never got a release listener.
However, a surface only uses explicit sync once it has received an
acquire fence. Without one (e.g. GPU drivers lacking native fence
support), SetExplicitSync() returns false and the buffer is attached
without a syncobj surface, so the compositor releases it with
wl_buffer.release. That event was dropped, buffers were never returned,
and the window stopped updating after a few frames.

Add the wl_buffer listener for kSyncobj buffers too, and record per
attach whether explicit sync was used. wl_buffer.release events are
ignored while it is, since their delivery is undefined then and the
release point signals the release instead.

[Glic DLP] Enforce screenshot protection in GlicPageContextFetcher

Prevent GlicPageContextFetcher from capturing or returning viewport
screenshots when enterprise screenshot protection or DataControlsRules
SCREENSHOT restrictions block screenshots on the target tab. Other tab
context fields (such as inner_text and annotated_page_content) continue
to be returned when only SCREENSHOT is blocked.

To suppress mid-fetch screenshot capture, progress listener callbacks,
redaction/encoding, and metadata population when policy changes while a
fetch is in flight, add ScreenshotOptions::IsScreenshotAllowedCallback
and enforce it in PageContextFetcher.

Reland "[bedrock]: Dual-mode Document PiP file-picker tucking test"

This is a reland of commit 20a0587df9f1e170ffe1dd8d1373cd524524517a.
Disable DocumentPictureInPictureWindowControllerTuckingBrowserTest on
linux wayland.

Original change's description:
> [bedrock]: Dual-mode Document PiP file-picker tucking test
>
> Run TuckOnOpenFilePicker against both Browser-backed and standalone
> Document PiP windows. Keep the fake picker open asynchronously to check
> that the PiP widget moves while the picker is open and returns to its
> original bounds after cancellation.
>
> Add a fixture hook for extra feature states and share BackendParamName
> across the dual-mode fixtures.
>
> Bug: 515252142
> Change-Id: I45e709b25125e15a77350674889192de542be3c2
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8678212

Reason for revert: TuckOnOpenFilePicker/{BrowserBacked,Standalone}
failed on linux-wayland-mutter-rel-tests and
linux-wayland-weston-rel-tests
(https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8770547).
Ozone/Wayland has no global screen coordinates, so the PiP window origin
stays at (0, 0) and CheckOriginSet() times out.

Changes since the original:
- document_picture_in_picture_window_controller_browsertest.cc: skip
  TuckOnOpenFilePicker when the Ozone platform does not support global
  screen coordinates, using the same supports_global_screen_coordinates
  check as the other PiP tests.
See the diff from patchset 1 to the latest patchset for the exact change
(patchsets after 1 differ only by the fix and commit-message edits).

Fix service worker state race in protocol-handler WPTs

In external/wpt/html/webappapis/system-state-and-capabilities/
the-navigator-object/resources/handler-tools.js, runTest passes
reg.installing to wait_for_state() after
service_worker_unregister_and_register() resolves. If the service worker
has already transitioned from installing to waiting or active before the
registration promise resolves, reg.installing is null and
wait_for_state() throws an error. Fall back to reg.waiting || reg.active
so wait_for_state() receives a valid ServiceWorker object regardless of
how quickly installation progresses.

Flaky test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2F%5C%3Achrome_wpt_tests%21webtest%3A%3Aexternal%2Fwpt%2Fhtml%2Fwebappapis%2Fsystem-state-and-capabilities%2Fthe-navigator-object%23protocol-handler-path.https.html?q=VHASH%3A346bc8eacb050998

parent

[X11] Make DeviceListCacheX11 and hotplug updates asynchronous

Browser UI-thread hangs in x11::Connection::WaitForResponse frequently
occur inside ui::DeviceListCacheX11::UpdateDeviceList when XInput
HierarchyEvent or DeviceChangedEvent notifications arrive while the X
server is slow to reply (for example during VT switches, suspend/resume,
or input device hotplug).

Convert DeviceListCacheX11::UpdateDeviceList to issue ListInputDevices
and XIQueryDevice asynchronously via x11::Future::OnResponse, coalescing
overlapping refresh requests onto the latest request pair while keeping
the previously cached device lists available until both replies arrive.
Update X11EventSource to refresh TouchFactory, DeviceDataManagerX11, and
X11HotplugEventHandler from the completion callback, and switch
X11HotplugEventHandler's "Device Node" lookup from synchronous XI1
OpenDevice/GetDeviceProperty calls to pipelined asynchronous XI2
XIGetProperty requests.

Sessions: Don't rebuild the session file mid-teardown

Chrome periodically rewrites the session file from scratch after many
updates. If this rewrite is triggered while a window is in the middle of
closing, the rebuild process snapshots the browser in an incomplete
teardown state, omitting tabs that were already destroyed. When Chrome
restarts, those tabs are permanently lost.

This change waits until the next scheduled save to do that rewrite,
ensuring snapshots are not taken during window teardown. Opening a new
window after the last one closed also drops any pending rewrite, so the
old session is never replaced with an incomplete one.

Found by a TLA+ model of session persistence; one of the fixes in
crrev.com/c/8746541.

93c7e75e2dc72e3b783f5acbec438cff4ad93ae1 [ diff ] 
[Contextual Tasks] Use weak pointers for async tasks in UiService

ContextualTasksUiService is a profile-keyed service that posts deferred
tasks when it intercepts navigations from the Contextual Tasks side
panel. OpenUrl() and OnSearchResultsNavigationInSidePanel() were bound
with raw BrowserWindowInterface* and ContextualTasksUIInterface*
pointers. If the window or side panel WebUI was destroyed before the
task ran, the task used freed memory in the browser process.

Bind base::WeakPtr instead: BrowserWindowInterface already exposes
GetWeakPtr(), and ContextualTasksUIInterface gains one backed by
ContextualTasksUI's existing factory. OpenUrl() returns early if the
window was destroyed, and OnSearchResultsNavigationInSidePanel() if
the WebUI was.

Bug: 573368237
Allow-Contextual-Tasks-Ui-Changes: Security fix for the legacy UI interface

Revert "[WebUI Toolbar] Focus the internal WebView when focusing the app menu"

This reverts commit 7568d83a3290f12b866dffae5585cda5b1c86436.

Reason for revert: Suspect for WebUIToolbarWebViewStabilityTest failures on mac

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Mac13%20Tests/43806/overview
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/mac26-arm64-rel-tests/6000/overview
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/mac15-x64-rel-tests/23289/overview

Original change's description:
> [WebUI Toolbar] Focus the internal WebView when focusing the app menu
>
> WebUIAppMenuControl::Focus() passed the WebUIToolbarWebView container
> to AccessiblePaneView::SetPaneFocus(). The container is not focusable,
> so FocusManager::AdvanceFocusIfNecessary() advanced focus into the
> internal WebView through tab traversal, which calls
> WebContents::FocusThroughTabTraversal() and makes the renderer focus
> the page's first focusable element (e.g. the reload button).
>
> The app menu focus request itself goes to the page over the WebUI
> toolbar Mojo pipe, which is not ordered with the tab traversal IPC.
> When the traversal arrived last, it moved focus off the app menu
> button right after the button reported focus, so a following ArrowDown
> or ArrowUp went to another toolbar button and the menu never opened.
> This made the following test flaky on all platforms:
> WebUIToolbarWebViewInteractiveTest.AppMenuOpensOnArrowKeysAndClearsFocusOnBlur
>
> Focus the internal WebView directly instead, which focuses it without
> tab traversal.
>
> Tested with UTR on the builders' bots, no retries:
> linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi: 9/40 failed before, 0/40 after.
> linux-arm64-rel-fyi: 4/40 failed before, 0/40 after.
>
> Fixed: 580360454
> Bug: 580641447

parent
ca4bfcec27ec9b433304b99c08788a9fff3c8c88 [ diff ] 
[ash] ui_test_utils: Compare window owner instead of Profile

IsBrowserShownForProfile() in ui_test_utils converted the AccountId
of the user presenting the browser window back to a Profile via
multi_user_util::GetProfileFromAccountId(), only to compare it with
the profile that the caller already matched against the browser's
profile.

Since FindAnyBrowser() only reaches this check for browsers whose
original profile matches the requested one, the check effectively
asks whether the browser window is teleported to another user's
desktop. ash::MultiUserWindowManager already knows both the window
owner and the presenting user as AccountIds, so compare them
directly and drop the Profile round trip. The helper is renamed to
IsBrowserShownForOwner() accordingly.

This removes a use of multi_user_util, which is being removed.

Synchronize PageActionType histogram variants with actual properties

Update PageActionType variants in histograms.xml to match the actual
PageActionProperties::histogram_name values defined in
page_action_properties_provider.cc. This includes adding missing
variants, updating obsolete ones, and providing helpful summaries for
each.

To prevent future drift, establish a LINT block pair linking the
variants in histograms.xml with the kPageActionProperties definition in
page_action_properties_provider.cc. Additionally, update the comment in
page_action_icon_type.h to clarify that PageActionType variants are
derived from the properties provider rather than the icon type enum.

Add a new unit test, PageActionPropertiesProviderTest, to
programmatically verify that the registered properties match the defined
histogram XML variants.

parent
38087e1ee387f7d7e82c98153438208712551538 [ diff ] 
PaintTiming: Move LCP performance entry emission and tracing to delegates

This CL is another step towards creating LCP/ICP entries at paint time.
It moves the multi-stage logic of first creating pending candidate data
(info for metrics, LCP entry) and then separately flushing this data to
the delegates (LCP manager, soft nav context). Currently this all
happens at presentation time, so the behavior doesn't change -- but this
sets things up to move the first stage to paint and the second stage
to presentation time, which will change behavior.

Details:
 - Delegates call the calculator's UpdateLargestContentfulPaint() to
   create the PendingCandidateData, moving this out of the LCP
   calculator. Each delegate then passes this data to a presentation
   time callback to flush the candidate data, invoking the provided
   metrics callback, emitting candidate trace events if needed, and
   finalizing and emitting the performance entry if needed.
 - Remove the Delegate's EmitLcpPerformanceEntry
 - Define soft FCP to be the first soft LCP. They could differ when the
   the first contentful paint's PaintTimingRecord::Node gets detached
   from the DOM, which will no longer be an issue once we create entries
   at paint time. Doing this ahead of time aligns the two versions now
   and simplifies the code, and it's made possible by the recent
   changes.

[TabStripModel] Add TabHandle overloads for single-tab state APIs

Add tabs::TabHandle overloads on TabStripModel for single-tab state
queries and mutations so observers and bridges do not need to convert
handles to indices:
- GetIndexOfTab(tabs::TabHandle)
- GetOpenerOfTab / SetOpenerOfTab
- SetTabBlocked / SetTabPinned
- IsTabCollapsed / IsTabInForeground / GetPersistableTabGroupForTab

Migrate TabListBridge callers (SetOpenerForTab, GetOpenerForTab,
GetIndexOfTab, PinTab, UnpinTab) to use the new overloads directly.

Original change's description:
> Set the URL when overriding language detection in tests
>
> TranslateAgent's --override-language-detection branch, used by
> TranslateManagerBrowserTest and other translate tests, registered the
> page without a URL. Since the driver started telling the Reading Mode
> side panel apart from the page by URL, the side panel's registration
> in such tests looked like a new main-frame registration. It replaced
> the page's TranslateAgent remote, so an in-flight TranslateFrame()
> completed as cancelled, OnPageTranslated() never reached observers,
> and TranslateWaiter timed out.

> finished loading after the translation started: 15/20 runs failed on
> linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi (17/20 with doubled timeouts) and 9/40 on

> re-enable TogglePresentationKeepsPdfTranslation on Linux MSan.

> 40x on x64 Linux (X11) and on linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi via UTR;

d20483bf3be07a5c4276fcd1712115a5368e2db7
parent
f0a16d79a74f3ed138667c2c4bcd465b99e04005 [ diff ] 
[iOS][TabGrid] Guard transitionItemForActiveCell against nil cells

UIKit's `indexPathsForVisibleItems` can include `selectedItemIndexPath`
while `cellForItemAtIndexPath:` returns `nil` (for example, near the
viewport edge, as seen in https://crbug.com/450903060). After
https://crrev.com/c/7280325 switched `TabGridTransitionHandler` to rely
on `!_tabGridCellItem` for the reduced-motion fallback, a `nil` cell in
`-transitionItemForActiveCell` produced a non-nil
`TabGridTransitionItem` with `originalFrame = CGRectZero`, risking a `0
/ 0 = NaN` `CALayerInvalidGeometry` crash in `GridToTabAnimation`.

Return `nil` from `-transitionItemForActiveCell` when `selectedIndex` is
`NSNotFound`, when `cell` is `nil`, or when `[cell snapshotFrame]` is
empty, and add unit tests covering `transitionLayout.activeCell`. Also
fix a minor comment typo and stray blank line in the touched method.

media: Remove ClearKeyCdm sleep and stop renewal timer loop

ClearKeyCdm::OnUpdateSuccess blocked the CDM thread for a full second to
check time and sleep syscalls, and ClearKeyCdm::TimerExpired kept
rescheduling renewal timers indefinitely after delivering the
license-renewal message. Remove the sleep check, only reschedule
periodic timers for output-protection polling, and re-enable
ECKEncryptedMediaTest.MessageTypeTest on ASan and LSan.

This cuts 3s across the three MessageTypeTest browser test runs, stops
in-flight renewal timers at teardown, and restores ASan and LSan test
coverage.

1a59eec420b3e01d3abaa96703eae68c09dc0a93 [ diff ] 
[contextual-tasks] Add ABA side panel query submission metrics

Adds ContextualTasksPanelController::OnQuerySubmitted(), called from
both query submission paths when the panel is showing the submitting
WebContents: ContextualTasksWebContentsUserData (AIM-hosted composebox)
and ContextualTasksComposeboxHandler (WebUI composebox, including
queries that skip recontextualization, such as visual-selection-only
queries). A query from a Contextual Tasks UI shown in a tab isn't
counted for the panel's session. The side panel coordinator counts
submitted queries per open session, mirroring how link clicks are
counted.

For sessions opened via the ABA search journey contextual cue
(EntrySource::kAbaOnRamp), this records:
- The ContextualTasks.SearchJourney.QuerySubmitted user action for each
  submitted query, so user-action sequences show follow-ups after
  ContextualTasks.SearchJourney.Restored.
- ContextualTasks.Session.QuerySubmitCount.AbaOnRamp when the session
  ends. The restored AI Overview turn isn't a submitted query, so 0
  means the side panel was closed without a follow-up.

The two submission paths come from different composeboxes, so a single
query is only counted once, assuming AIM doesn't answer a ClientToAim
query with an OnSubmitQueryRequest.

cbde11b3ad6f45cdee73cfb2a0782db1ace8877d
parent
090987d5d0162013923cf695c17cd8f78c974b7c [ diff ] 
[ProfilesReordering] Avoid OS file drag when dragging profile cards

When dragging a profile card by its avatar image in the Profile Picker,
Blink's DragController::DraggableNode() previously matched the inner
<img> element (which is draggable by default) before reaching the outer
<profile-card draggable="true"> host element. This initiated an image
drag (kDragSourceActionImage) that populated virtual file and URL data
(CFSTR_FILEDESCRIPTORW, CFSTR_FILECONTENTS, UniformResourceLocatorW) in
the Windows IDataObject, causing File Explorer (e.g. an open Downloads
folder) on the Windows 11 taskbar to activate and open when hovering
near the bottom of the screen.

Set draggable="false" on the profile avatar <img> in profile-card so
initiating a drag anywhere on the card drags the <profile-card> element
directly without image/file payload data, and clear
event.dataTransfer.items in DragDropReorderTileListDelegate.onDragStart_
so internal tile reordering never exports drag items to external OS drop
targets.

Fixed: 581964010
Test: out/Default/browser_tests --gtest_filter=ProfilePickerTest.*

parent
d8b694f8d53c244305a1221a7950350ebed39aeb [ diff ] 
Add CEReactions to select/option IDLs

https://github.com/whatwg/html/pull/12263 added [CEReactions] to
HTMLSelectElement's selectedIndex and value, HTMLOptionsCollection's
selectedIndex, and HTMLOptionElement's selected, since setting them can
clone custom elements into selectedcontent elements. This part of the
spec PR was missed when the rest of it was implemented.

This makes the existing custom-elements/reactions tests for these
attributes pass, as well as the test in
https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/pull/64816.

This patch also fixes a DCHECK failure in
MenuListSelectType::OptionToBeShown when calling form.reset(), which
was hit by the new test. HTMLSelectElement::ResetImpl updated option
mutation observers before last_on_change_option_ was updated. This
part of the change is not web exposed.

parent
09261bc0ed032ca09a8466cd85f4d1d5c67e1104 [ diff ] 
PaintTiming: Add LargestContentfulPaintCalculator::PendingMetricsData

This is another step towards creating LCP/ICP entries and metrics data
at paint time. This CL adds PendingMetricsData, which encapsulates the
data needed at presentation time. In this CL, we compute this data at
presentation time, but we'll eventually compute it at paint time and
propagate to presentation time (via Delegates). Splitting this out will
isolate behavioral changes in a future patch, as this is a essentially a
pure refactor**.

**This also fixes a long-standing edge case where in the event of a
tie between image and text size for the per-frame largest candidate,
we would use the text, but everywhere else (i.e. metrics) uses the
image.

Revert "[Settings 2026 Refresh] Add 2-column card styling to settings-radio-group"

This reverts commit 0c240469a1e19114176392f2799921e7bad5a700.

Reason for revert: We are no longer using this design, as mocks have
changed. The new design is not compatible with this, and it would be
more effort to alter it than to revert and start over.

Merge conflicts resolved after:
- Polymer-to-Lit and PrefService migrations of controlled-radio-button,
  settings-radio-group, and settings-cookies-page.
- Removal of the Related Website Sets UI (crrev.com/c/8437896).
- Removal of security_page_v2 (crrev.com/c/8500344).

Original change's description:
> [Settings 2026 Refresh] Add 2-column card styling to settings-radio-group
>

7767cc098c5d17257cc68da6876e088b89665988 [ diff ] 
Update CDP attribution UMA histograms for command-based TTL

- Replace Enterprise.CdpAttribution.UploadSecurityEvent.BuildLatency
  with Enterprise.CdpAttribution.UploadSecurityEvent.BuildLatency2,
  switching from UmaHistogramCustomTimes (1ms-100ms) to
  UmaHistogramCustomMicrosecondsTimes (1us-10ms, 50 buckets) since
  building EventInitiatorInfo is pure in-memory UI-thread work.
- Add Enterprise.CdpAttribution.UploadSecurityEvent.Category
  (CdpSessionCategory: kDevToolsUi, kExternalPipe, kExternalWebSocket)
  to break down attributed lookups by CDP session category.

Observe foreground changes from SettingsWindowFinderWin
📋 Metadata
commit
0afc0887b3c6d8d077df917267d2c5990e7ae984
[ log ]
author
Foromo Daniel Soromou <koretadaniel@chromium.org>
Tue Nov 10 19:24:26 2026
committer

Tue Nov 10 19:24:26 2026
tree
382eab8bcf0d3db54cd7132ae300be867c9dfbbb
parent
b5faf54908f88ea9ea77625faeebf72de29b8f7a [ diff ] 
Observe foreground changes from SettingsWindowFinderWin

The annotated setter controller watched the foreground window through
its own Win32WindowInspector: a second SetWinEventHook, plus a static
map from hook handle to inspector so the C callback could find its
instance. SettingsWindowFinderWin already does this job for the Settings
window's location, visibility and move/size events, and already owns the
global instance that routes the out-of-context callback to the active
finder.

Move the foreground observation there, as
StartObservingForegroundChanges and StopObservingForegroundChanges
beside their location counterparts, and dispatch EVENT_SYSTEM_FOREGROUND
through the finder's existing WinEventCallback. The hook is the only one
on the finder that is neither scoped to the Settings process nor
skipping Chrome's own: the owner needs to learn that focus moved to any
window, including a Chrome window or its own bubble. As before, the
callback carries no window and the owner asks ::GetForegroundWindow()
for the current one. A newer finder taking over the global instance now
stands the older one's foreground hook down too, and destruction
releases it.

WindowInspector shrinks to its four queries, so Win32WindowInspector no
longer needs a sequence checker, a destructor or the hook map, and the
controller's fake inspector loses its trigger: the controller test
drives foreground changes through the fake finder, as it already does
for location changes. The finder's tests cover the new dispatch, its
independence from the location observation, a callback that stops the
observation reentrantly, and eviction by a newer finder.

tree
91a94a571ccc29438cddfcf94f5f3a79dccdfa27
parent
f2323168a44f1d592400c49469be37b7392c6a73 [ diff ] 
PaintTiming: Replace ImageRecord first animated frame tracking with HeapTimeTicks

To support overriding the metrics timestamp with the first animated
image frame timestamp, ImagePaintTimingDetector stores the relevant
ImageRecords and hands them off to PaintTiming at the end of the frame
to set their first animated frame time when the frame is presented.

This CL changes this to use a HeapTimeTicks (new) to track the
timestamp instead of using the ImageRecord around, with the goals
of minimizing the state needed to track from paint to presentation
and (eventually) having ImageRecord's lifetime end at the first
sufficiently loaded paint.

Details
 - Add HeapTimeTicks, which is a GC-ed wrapper for a base::TimeTicks
 - Rename PaintTimingRecord methods to (Set)MetricsTimestampOverride to
   reflect what the value is for.
 - First video frame initializes the override with the actual first
   timestamp since it is known at creation time. Animated images
   initialize this (to TimeTicks()) if it hasn't already been
   initialized, which also prevents us from continuing to ask for
   presentation time if there are multiple non-loaded paints for the
   image.

e543dd11d97e9d5148975678540e11fbe59bc995
parent
09c404f33b13cd51074dc74ba459d27d1da971c8 [ diff ] 
Allows immersive fullscreen to keep top container in BrowserView ...

instead of always moving it to the overlay view.

Currently in immersive fullscreen (used by ChromeOS and macOS), the top
container is always moved to a separate overlay view, which is outside
of BrowserView. This incurs a few limitations, e.g.,

- No Toolbar-height Side Panel.
- The Organizer Panel cannot extend to full browser height when "Always
  Show Toolbar in Full Screen" is enabled.

This CL changes that so that the top container can switch dynamically
between living in BrowserView vs. overlay view. The intent user journeys
are:

- when "Always Show Toolbar in Full Screen" is enabled, moves top
  container into BrowserView. The non-fullscreen layout is reused.
- when "Always Show Toolbar in Full Screen" is disabled, moves top
  container into the overlay view.

No behavior change yet: nothing overrides
ShouldMoveTopContainerToOverlayView() yet. The Mac immersive fullscreen
v2 controller will be in a follow-up, which also adds tests.

Demo video of toggling "Always Show Toolbar", after everything is hooked
up:
https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjY1MjQ5NDc5MTgzNTY0OHw5YWMzZjNlNy1mOA

Design doc:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1uWizKU_3zAexeWqm3is4L1ZCfH0HnOmxnIKxcuSRjUs

[iOS][Composebox] Enable server-side state across EarlGrey tests

kComposeboxServerSideState has launched by default on both iPhone and
iPad, so Composebox, Assistant, and Cobrowse EarlGrey suites should
exercise server-driven tool and attachment state rather than skipping
tests or forcing the local fallback path.

- Remove the eight server-side-state skip guards in ComposeboxTestCase
  and ComposeboxEligiblityTestCase along with the now-unused
  +[ComposeboxAppInterface isServerSideStateEnabled] helper.
- Stop force-disabling kComposeboxServerSideState in Assistant and
  Cobrowse EarlGrey test configurations.
- Track attached and deleted FileInfo entries in
  MockIOSContextualSearchService and wait for tab upload completion in
  testNoTabAttachmentsInImageGeneration so InputStateModel observes
  context changes deterministically during UI tests.

0ffbd1fbc92dcc84bb62cba5561c55f365124e0c
parent
eace48ea1dc694a63c60d8dcb228f74dde4c977e [ diff ] 
[Focus] Cache TabGroupView Minimum Size and Crossover Width

Previously, TabGroupView layouts cached preferred size via
LayoutManagerBase while computing minimum size and crossover width live
from the current focus state. When focus changed and the unpinned tab
container recalculated layout before TabGroupView was invalidated, or
while tab-closing mode width overrides remained active, a stale cached
preferred group width could be paired with live focused minimum and
crossover widths, causing minimum width to exceed preferred width and
crashing during layout width clamping.

To fix this, TabGroupView layouts now rely on LayoutManagerBase to cache
minimum size alongside preferred size, and TabGroupViewHorizontalLayout
caches crossover width until layout invalidation. In addition,
TabGroupView invalidates affected groups on non-animated focus changes
and avoids starting a competing layout animation when auto-expanding a
collapsed group into focus mode, while TabStripCollectionController
exits tab-closing mode on focus transitions.

TAG=agy
CONV=4762310a-a80a-42a5-a741-8f19f441c870

a17ebe9ee07299a87795c38654d77e0f0f008d52
parent
4b3dfe9063ac893aafc7dcffdabe0a0a0e6daf02 [ diff ] 
[OmniboxEverywhere] Preserve AUMID during taskbar pin migration

MigrateTaskbarPins rewrites the AppUserModelId of every taskbar
shortcut targeting chrome.exe or chrome_proxy.exe to the value from
GetExpectedAppId(). GetExpectedAppId() did not model Omnibox
Everywhere, so chrome_proxy.exe --omnibox-everywhere pins were given
the browser's AUMID, causing a second taskbar button when launching
from the pin.

Move the Omnibox Everywhere app name to chrome_constants so
shell_integration can compute the Omnibox Everywhere AUMID without
depending on the feature, and use it in GetExpectedAppId(). This also
repairs pins already clobbered by earlier migrations.

[🌷] Deflake RadioButtonWithIconRenderTest#testRadioButtonWithIcon

RadioButtonWithIconRenderTest launched BlankUiTestActivity in
@BeforeClass before the parameterized constructor configured
NightModeTestUtils, causing the activity to retain stale night-mode
theme resources or race with asynchronous recreation during @Before.

NightModeTestUtils sets the night mode, and re-enable the test.

b9579b44692cbc125a48ad9a6e55e6b25f45bc13 [ diff ] 
Attribute enterprise interstitial reporting events to EventInitiatorInfo

Update ReportingEventRouter::OnUrlFilteringInterstitial(),
OnSecurityInterstitialShown(), and OnSecurityInterstitialProceeded() to
accept std::optional<EventInitiatorInfo> and call
MaybeSetEventInitiator() instead of MaybeSetExtensionInitiator().

Update MaybeTriggerUrlFilteringInterstitialEvent(),
MaybeTriggerSecurityInterstitialShownEvent(), and
MaybeTriggerSecurityInterstitialProceededEvent() in
chrome/browser/interstitials/enterprise_util.cc to query
GetActiveEventInitiatorInfo(web_contents) instead of
GetActiveExtensionInitiatorInfo(web_contents), so interstitial events
attributed to CDP, Gemini in Chrome, or extension actors include the
populated Event.initiator field (and appear in

[iOS][actor] Remove UnknownTool variant from per-tool UMA histograms

Stop emitting per-tool UMA histograms for ToolType::kUnknown and remove
the UnknownTool variant from IOSActorUiToolName. Unknown or unset tool
requests fail during tool creation and continue to be recorded in the
aggregate IOS.Actor.ExecutionEngine.Action.ResultCode histogram,
matching Desktop behavior.

Also update RecordToolTimings, RecordToolActionResultCode, and
StartAsyncJournalEntry to accept ToolType directly instead of raw tool
name strings, moving ToolTypeToToolName into actor_tool_types.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[IOS.Actor.Tools.ActionResultCode.UnknownTool]=Removed; unknown tools are only recorded in aggregate IOS.Actor.ExecutionEngine.Action.ResultCode.
OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[IOS.Actor.Tools.ExecutionDuration.UnknownTool]=Removed; unknown tools fail creation before execution.
OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[IOS.Actor.Tools.PageStabilization.UnknownTool]=Removed; unknown tools fail creation before page stabilization.

[Immersive Mode] Make the browser immersive mode persist across tab switches & navigations

After: https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NDg5MzI0NTcxOTk2OTc5Mnw1OTRkMDcxOC04Yw

When ExclusiveAccessManager is constructed, it registers a FullscreenManagerDelegate on mFullscreenManager (FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase).

The fix in FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase in this CL allows:

1) Switching tabs (Ctrl + tab)
2) Clicking a link
3) Opening a new tab (Ctrl + T)
4) Closing the tab (Ctrl + W)

to NOT exit us out of immersive mode.

Design Doc:
go/clank-immersive-mode-button-design-doc

00bdc6234380acf488254f13a732a18100ed796c
parent
5392790802b63e0f0c8cae54da9a48edace7ba21 [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Use flat_hash_map for HistoryInfoMap

- Migrate HistoryInfoMap from std::unordered_map to absl::flat_hash_map
  to eliminate per-entry node allocations and pointer chasing, and
  right-size RebuildFromHistory's kMaxReserve to 448 (~108 KiB) to cover
  typical histories with zero rehashes without over-allocating inline
  slots on small profiles.
- Replace the O(N) GURL-scanning DeleteURL() methods with O(1)
  URLIndexPrivateData::RemoveRowFromIndex(URLID) for history deletions
  (also fixing deletion of credential-bearing URLs whose credentials are
  stripped on indexing), and avoid a redundant hash lookup in
  RemoveRowWordsFromIndex().
- In Release benchmarks, this cuts container allocations to 1 (-18.9%
  container PA memory at Windows mean N = 448 and -3.2 KiB post-GC
  .text), speeds up end-to-end OnHistoryDeletions(500) by 5.14x (-34.3
  ms), TrimHistoryIdsPool() by 1.33x, Clear() by 1.16x, and
  UpdateRecentVisits() by 1.48x at 20k URLs.[1]

[1]
https://yuje.users.x20web.corp.google.com/generated/history_info_map_flat_hash_map_benchmark_2026-10-30.html

   `OnFileAttachedEnterpriseConnector` on Android, so policy changes

[ttc] Add TtcDebugToolsService

ec79d012e54a4fcde81b55ea8aaa098bafd3c3d3
[ log ]
author

committer

Tue Nov 10 19:01:58 2026
tree
c663ea172a4711987f001cb804c2f4fecfab567b
parent
583d3113a6b52971fdcff8a08136c22859154c60 [ diff ] 
[ttc] Add TtcDebugToolsService

Adds the profile-keyed service that holds the debug record of the most
recent TTC session for the debug tools. BeginSession() starts a new
TtcDebugSession, replacing (and ending) the previous one; EndSession()
marks it ended while keeping its data for the page. Records live in
memory only and are dropped on shutdown. Observers can subscribe to
session changes.

The service only exists while both ttc::kTtc and ttc::kTtcDebugTools
are enabled. The audio kept per session is capped at 64 MB.

Nothing starts sessions yet.

Part 8 of the chrome://ttc-internals series. Design: go/ttc-internals

tree
fb763382b428e50819cd39451f164d335fd62d23
parent
6a7cd002ff09efdd71f48f356bdd821cb52f20b0 [ diff ] 
[ASI] Gate the visual guide and annotated setters consistently

Consistently restrict both the visual guide and the annotated default
browser setters to Windows 11. Prior to this change, the annotated
setter was built only on Windows and fell back to shell integration
below Windows 11, while the visual guide setter was built on all desktop
platforms and could be selected anywhere (resulting in a navigation to
an empty URL on non-Windows platforms).

Consolidate this restriction by routing both guided setters through a
single IsSetterTypeSupported() check in GetDefaultBrowserSetterType().
This allows both options to consistently fall back to standard shell
integration on platforms outside of Windows 11. To support this unified
gating, compile the core setter logic on all desktop platforms while
retaining Windows-specific controller implementations only on Windows.

NO_IFTTT=This is a comment update only

Avoid use-after-free in WidgetInputHandlerImpl::DispatchEvent

Dispatching the original event for a gesture can trigger nested
execution that destroys the WidgetInputHandlerImpl instance (for
example, closing a popup).

Hold a scoped_refptr to WidgetInputHandlerManager and check that
WidgetInputHandlerImpl is still alive with a WeakPtr before proceeding
to dispatch the gesture event.

[Actor] Validate root-to-view coordinate transforms in tool actions
📋 Metadata

311afc41aa62f96371db31ed69278413a7b7a6c2
[ log ]
author
Min Qin <qinmin@chromium.org>
Tue Nov 10 18:55:55 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Tue Nov 10 18:55:55 2026
tree
6d88a52ab3f3ccc0b31785ec17b8d94eaa34d49a
parent
6c89fa6d0e90add7bfbab73f72c4e7e72cb979f2 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Validate root-to-view coordinate transforms in tool actions

When converting root viewport DIP coordinates to a child frame's
view coordinate space,
ChildFrameInputHelper::TransformRootPointToViewCoordSpace
previously fell back to returning the untransformed root coordinate
if TransformPointToCoordSpaceForView failed. For cross-origin
subframe tool actions where frame resolution uses document tokens
(such as DragAndRelease with a DOM node start target and coordinate
end target), a failed hit-test transform could dispatch
untransformed root coordinates as subframe-local coordinates.

Introduce TryTransformRootPointToViewCoordSpace to return
std::nullopt when coordinate transformation to a target view fails,
and reject Actor tool invocations with
kFrameLocationChangedSinceObservation instead of using
untransformed points.

Ash: Move wallpaper to a client-owned widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The wallpaper controller of each display already owned its widget and
still closes it at once when the display's root window shuts down, so
teardown does not change.

[Enterprise][Proxy] Separate proxy, PvD, and origin test servers

Previously, EnterpriseProxyBrowserTestBase multiplexed the CONNECT
proxy, /.well-known/pvd endpoint, and target origins onto a single
EmbeddedTestServer tunneling back to itself. Because all roles shared
one port and lifecycle, tests could not spin up multiple independent
proxies (e.g. for failover, ProxyOverrideRules/PAC precedence, or route
switches), shut down a proxy without taking down PvD/origin endpoints,
or cleanly verify whether traffic bypassed the proxy vs. never reached
the origin.

In particular, isolating pvd_server_ from the proxy and origin servers
is needed to test NetworkService request pausing end-to-end: follow-up
tests can hold /.well-known/pvd responses mid-flight on pvd_server_,
assert that concurrent navigations touch neither the proxy nor
origin_server_ while paused, and then release the PvD response to verify
the held navigation resumes through the newly provisioned proxy.

Split the fixture into dedicated, thread-safe HTTPS test servers:
- Extract a standalone TestConnectProxy helper (test_connect_proxy.h/cc)
  wrapping EmbeddedTestServer::EnableConnectProxy() for
  proxy1.example.com and additional proxies created via
  CreateProxyServer(), scoping leaf cert SANs per proxy hostname,
  tunneling strictly origin_server_ destinations, and supporting
  per-proxy 407 auth/realm config, tunneled target tracking, GetURL(),
  and independent Shutdown().
- Serve /.well-known/pvd configurations and overrides on pvd_server_.
- Serve target and direct-bypass web pages on origin_server_.
- Update EnterpriseProxyErrorBrowserTest to verify proxy_url against
  default_proxy_server().GetURL() (including loadTimeDataRaw on
  rendered error pages) instead of the PvD server URL.

Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.mac:mac26-arm64-rel-tests|browser_tests
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.memory:Linux ASan LSan Tests (1)|browser_tests
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-14-arm64-rel|android_browsertests
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-10-x86-nofieldtrial-rel|android_browsertests

[Glic] Route Chrome menu Cut/Copy/Paste to WebView, Textfield, or Label

When a secondary WebContents (such as a side panel like Glic or a WebUI
dialog) is focused, selecting Cut, Copy, or Paste from the Chrome
three-dot menu previously fell through to calling
focused_view->AcceleratorPressed() directly because the active tab's
RenderWidgetHostView did not have focus.

Calling AcceleratorPressed() directly on the hosting WebView bypassed
normal accelerator table validation, triggering an unhandled hotkey
crash in Glic (crbug.com/577212342). While crrev.com/c/8662381 prevented
the fatal NOTREACHED crash, the command remained a silent no-op.
Furthermore, calling AcceleratorPressed() on non-text native views
(such as Buttons) could inadvertently invoke unrelated click handlers
(crbug.com/582411426).

This CL:
1. Queries the active child/owned widget's FocusManager in
   BrowserView::CutCopyPaste() if one is active, ensuring focused views
   in bubbles and modal dialogs are handled.
2. Routes cut/copy/paste directly to the hosting views::WebView's
   WebContents when focused_view is or belongs to a WebView, unifying
   handling for the active tab, docked DevTools, side panels, and WebUI.
3. Restricts the native view fallback to views::Textfield and
   selectable views::Label, preventing edit accelerators from triggering
   unrelated views.
4. Adds browser tests covering WebViews, active child widgets,
   Textfields, selectable Labels, and Buttons.

Fix paint invalidation for content inside visibility:hidden ancestor

When a LayoutObject is visibility:hidden, we will try to short-circuit
paint invalidation. However, if an object has already signaled that
its subtree needs paint invalidation, then we should honor that rather
than short-circuiting.

parent
c10e79fc7ed17fbe0e0781f793d9f1a04ffb0da9 [ diff ] 
[net] Remove UDP GRO and cap recvmmsg reads at 16 packets

Now that batched UDP reads via recvmmsg (QuicUseReadMultiple) have
launched by default, remove the unused UDP Generic Receive Offload
(UDP_GRO) implementation and related plumbing:

- Remove features::kEnableUdpGro, its WebView production flag entry, the
  Linux network sandbox UDP_GRO setsockopt allowlist rule,
  extra_socket_defines.h, UDPSocketPosix GRO socket configuration and
  superpacket slicing logic, GRO unit tests, and the
  Net.UDPSocketPosix.GroPacketsRead and
  Net.UDPSocketPosix.SetSocketOptionGroResult histograms.
- Remove the GRO-only 64KB kMinimumReadMultipleBufferSize requirement.
- Cap the maximum number of packets read per recvmmsg call at 16 in both
  UDPSocketPosix (kMaxMmsgMessages) and QuicChromiumPacketReader
  (kNumPacketsToRead), reducing per-session QUIC read buffer allocations
  from ~66KB to ~23.5KB.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Removed in 2026-11 along with UDP GRO support.

Bug: 515333601
Test: net_unittests --gtest_filter=UDPSocketTest.*:*ReadMultiple*

d1a49a3e85414ee9e58aa494c13c4a599e16e81e [ diff ] 
[PDF] Reject re-entrancy in PDFiumPage::Unload()

Trying to unload in the middle of an unload is a bonehead maneuver. Add
a new `is_unloading_` bit to check for this and make re-entrant calls
into no-ops.

Also set `available_` to false for the same duration for good measure.
Check `is_unloading_` in MarkAvailable() to avoid unexpected changes to
this member variable.

Also mark `engine_` as const along the way.

parent
5608e466df30c97229cf14568e8f8ea1ef250fc0 [ diff ] 
[WebUI LLBC] Add missing permission chip icons in WebUI toolbar

With the WebUI toolbar, the permission request chip showed only its
text for clipboard, MIDI, storage access, local network, keyboard
and pointer lock and most other permission types. Only camera,
microphone, location and notifications had icons.

WebUIPermissionChip sent the raw C++ name of the chip's vector icon
(e.g. "kContentPasteIcon") over mojo, and permission_chip.ts
translated only a handful of those names to iconset ids. Every
other name mapped to '' and no icon was rendered. The indicator
chip and blocked/quiet icons had the same gap, and the shared
iconset lacked SVGs for most of these permissions.

Resolve the icon in C++ instead: WebUIPermissionChip maps each
gfx::VectorIcon a chip can show (rounded and old variants, blocked
icons, and the ambient sign-in overrides) to a webui-toolbar-shared
iconset id, and sends that id. Unknown icons hit
DUMP_WILL_BE_NOTREACHED() so new request types can't silently lose
their icon. The renderer now uses the id as is. Add the missing
SVGs to the shared iconset, generated from the same .icon files the
native chip paints.

A new parameterized unit test checks that every request type's icon
and blocked icon map to an id registered in icons.ts.

tree
5cd375e33f3d0e64c912be06822e1ad3801b8dc1
parent
1823da76aec57bd9c3d010a100929d99aa396087 [ diff ] 
mac: Read Full Keyboard Access from preferences on mode change

BridgedContentView observes the com.apple.KeyboardUIModeDidChange
distributed notification and then queries
-[NSApplication isFullKeyboardAccessEnabled]. That value is backed by a
HIToolbox cache which is invalidated by an observer of the same
notification. When Chrome's observer runs first, it reads the stale
cached value, so the FocusManager's keyboard_accessible state lags one
toggle behind the system setting.

Add ui::IsFullKeyboardAccessEnabled(), which reads AppleKeyboardUIMode
directly from the user preferences (the same preference and bit
HIToolbox uses), and use it in BridgedContentView and
NativeWidgetNSWindowBridge. ScopedFakeFullKeyboardAccess now overrides
the new function as well.

results panel over the Lens UI, so keeping LVF up until the page reaches
50% load progress reads as a frozen screen. Dismisses the Lens UI as soon
as the Lens Web page starts loading instead, saving ~1s of perceived
latency in local testing.

parent
b84576a1d445f6b387d7d03cab26f8c84238d48d [ diff ] 
[Safe Browsing] Record regular files named *.app in archive analysis

On macOS, `UpdateArchiveAnalyzerResultsWithFile()` skipped every archive
entry whose extension is `.app`, assuming it is an application bundle
directory. It did not check whether the file is actually a directory, so
regular files with a name ending in `.app` (e.g. a non-Mach-O file in a
DMG, or any file in a ZIP/RAR/7z) were omitted from the analysis report,
potentially allowing malicious executable to evade Safe Browsing
detection. This CL only skips `.app` entries that are actually
directories.

parent
672651f9816402729dc25bda29bffc92b4a2b666 [ diff ] 
[win] Reuse resolved display paths for monitor IDs

Display enumeration queries the configuration separately for monitor
metadata and ID generation. During display changes, these queries can
observe different target mappings for the same monitor, potentially
contributing to duplicate IDs and the CHECK in
GetDisplayInfosFromSystem.

Resolve the path from the existing monitor information and reuse it for
both metadata and ID generation. This removes a re-query race window,
keeping each monitor's ID consistent with its resolved path. Preserve
the device-name fallback when the path is unavailable, without querying
again.

[TabData] Pull tab audio notifications out of OnTabChanged()

Tab audio notifications were part of the OnTabChanged() notifications
but that function is also called for many other tab changed reasons
that it is difficult to understand who needs to observe this function.

This CL breaks audio notifications out of this function so that it
will be clear who needs to subscribe for audio notifications and
eventually deprecate the OnTabChanged() method.

ea19ee97cb4a35c29077f8d3b2b8e1fb0c0ee964

tree
9ed05bc6ffe9c97d11bab8beba6c919462bc2395
parent
22d720dc4e95643cf030dd79d9e37949ca52f433 [ diff ] 
[a11y] Add OffsetType enum and optional offset types to AXTreeData

Selection endpoints are sent from Blink to the browser as (node id,
offset) pairs, without saying whether the offset is a child index (a
tree position) or a character offset (a text position). Consumers then
have to guess from the shape of the anchor and focus nodes. This CL
adds the type information to AXTreeData so that follow-up CLs can stop
guessing.

This CL only adds the AXTreeData plumbing. Nothing sets or reads the
new fields yet, so there is no behavior change:

- Adds the ax::mojom::OffsetType enum (kChild, kText) and
  ToString(OffsetType).
- Adds optional sel_anchor_offset_type and sel_focus_offset_type to
  AXTreeData with Mojo traits and round-trip tests. An unset value
  means the type is unknown, and consumers keep inferring it as they do
  today.
- Updates AXTreeCombiner::Combine() and
  AXEventGenerator::OnTreeDataChanged() to carry and compare the new
  fields along with the selection offsets.

AX-Relnotes: n/a.

Reland "[iOS] Move omnibox, toolbar and NTP EG test disables to expectations"

This is a reland of commit bcae6df3058c740c4b10fdf5155ab9dcc7ff74

It was reverted because the strict `[ iPad iOS27 ] [ Failure ]`
expectation for `NTPHomeTestCase/testMinimumHeight` failed on the
iPad (A16) 27.0 bots, where the test passes. That line is removed;
the general `[ Pass Failure ]` line still covers the test.

Original change's description:
> [iOS] Move omnibox, toolbar and NTP EG test disables to expectations
>
> Migrates the `FLAKY_`, `DISABLED_` and `MAYBE_` method prefixes and
> the in-body `EARL_GREY_TEST_DISABLED` gates in omnibox, toolbar, NTP,
> popup menu, fullscreen, find in page and overscroll EG tests into
> `ios/testing/test_expectations.txt`, following the team rule (flaky ->
> `[ Pass Failure ]`, always fails -> `[ Failure ]`).
>
> - Migrated tests get a `[ Pass Failure ]` line with the tags of their
>   original scope, so they cannot turn the bots red.
> - Tests that failed every run on a config also get a strict
>   `[ Failure ]` line for that config; tests that failed everywhere are
>   marked `[ Failure ]` only.
> - Verified on iOS 27: up to 100 runs per test on iPad (A16) and iPhone
>   16 simulators, up to 50 runs per test on real iPad 9th Gen and
>   iPhone 16 Pro. Configs without results get no strict line.
>   Tests that passed every run stay listed here and will be re-enabled
>   in a separate CL.
> - `PopupMenuTestCase/testOpenAndCloseToolsMenu` was gated on iPad in
>   compact width; the `[ iPad ]` tag is broader since width can't be
>   expressed.
> - The iPhone `XCTSkip` in `testFindInPageSwitchingTabs` becomes an
>   `[ iPhone ]` line with its bug.
> - Tests disabled without a bug use tracking bug crbug.com/476358542.

33bacf695eab432d167e1d546edf691d427b9e71 [ diff ] 
Use designated initializers for remaining positional callers

ComponentRegistration and ComponentInfo are aggregates now, so these
callers compile through C++20 parenthesized aggregate initialization.
That ties each argument to member declaration order rather than to a
constructor signature. If a member were inserted or reordered, the
arguments could bind to the wrong fields without a compile error. The
risk is highest in the policy browsertest, which passes five adjacent
bools, including the supports_group_policy_enable_component_updates
value under test.

Name the fields explicitly. Fields that keep their default values are
omitted. The values themselves don't change.

[glic] Add GlicModal C++ API and Desktop Views implementation

Features in Glic and Chrome (such as experimental opt-in and Skills)
need to present tab-scoped WebUI modals across both Desktop and Android,
which previously required each feature to maintain its own Views
DialogDelegate, JNI bridge, and Android ThinWebView coordinator.

Introduce glic::GlicModal in //chrome/browser/glic/browser_ui:modal
to provide a single cross-platform entry point (GlicModal::Show) for
tab-scoped WebUI modals, along with its Desktop Views implementation
(glic_modal_desktop.cc) and browser tests:
- Wraps a views::WebView in a views::DialogDelegate managed by
  tabs::TabDialogManager, with configurable content-driven sizing
  (size_to_content), optional native loading_view + ShowContent(),
  available-size callbacks, 24dp rounded corners, and accessibility
  title announcements.
- Includes a temporary Android stub for GlicModal::Show before the
  Android JNI implementation is added in the next commit.

Demo: http://go/glic-demos:glic-modal-desktop

b3fe913b3b8e8f7e31ac8dbc3630d7a1cc056007 [ diff ] 
[webview] Gate prefetch intercept skip on URLLoaderFactoryType

The prefetch shortcut in ShouldNotInterceptRequest was gated on
web_contents_key_ == std::nullopt to identify profile-initiated
prefetches. The same nullopt is also passed for service worker and
shared worker subresource factories, so a request on those factories
that carried a Sec-Purpose: prefetch header was incorrectly treated as a
profile-initiated prefetch and skipped the embedder
shouldInterceptRequest callback.

Plumb an explicit is_for_profile_initiated_prefetch flag from
WillCreateURLLoaderFactory to AwProxyingURLLoaderFactory, set only for
URLLoaderFactoryType::kPrefetch factories not bound to a frame, and gate
the skip on that instead. The decision is moved into a static helper so
it can be unit-tested directly.

Bug: b:499176175
Test: autoninja -C out/Default android_webview_unittests

f1e29ed4a2bd262b15c96a652113bdc11a895777 [ diff ] 
[glic] Stream conversation activity to GlicActivityManager

When the Glic web client starts or stops generating a conversation
response, GlicActivityManager needs to track that activity so the
Activity Hub bubble and split button reflect background work, unseen
responses, and errors—including when response events arrive before a
conversation ID is registered on the instance.

Introduce an internal GlicInstanceObserver interface in
chrome/browser/glic/common/ (scoped to :private_types) and notify
observers from GlicInstanceImpl for visibility changes, conversation
registration, response start/stop (via Host::InstanceDelegate), web
client resets, task tab additions, and destruction. GlicActivityManager
privately implements GlicInstanceObserver, subscribes to new instances
via GlicInstanceCoordinatorImpl::AddInstanceCreatedCallback, buffers
pre-registration state per InstanceId, clears in-progress state on
user-cancelled responses or web client resets, and detaches observations
on Shutdown().

Bug: b:565017978

tree

parent
251cd8cbe617bf45792ec0dc787a8afa8b2b16f4 [ diff ] 
[Glic] End the session on synchronous terminal updates

Synchronous terminal updates (COMPLETE, STOPPED, FAILED) end the task
for the server. Chrome now stops live work and ends the session when
a request returns a terminal task update.

- A ContinueActuationRequest that is refused synchronously (for example,
  not opted in or no browser window) still gets FAILED. Chrome now also
  stops the yielded task with StopOwnedLiveWork() and destroys the
  handler.
- A malformed or unknown request mid-task (missing task metadata,
  payload not set, monostate payload, or a proto-level version mismatch
  or missing payload) still gets FAILED. Chrome now also stops live work
  and ends the session. The version mismatch and missing payload replies
  are built before the request reaches a handler, and used sender
  sequence number 0, so the server dropped them as stale while a session
  was live. If a handler is live, they now take its next sequence number.
  On Android, if a Trigger still waits for a background tab on the same
  context, it is cancelled too, so it can't start the task later.
- A duplicate start request while work runs (Trigger or Continue while
  action execution runs, Trigger while a Glic session exists, or
  ExecuteActions while any task runs, including a Trigger after YIELD
  while the Glic session is live) is dropped: Chrome replies empty
  (std::nullopt), records kTaskAlreadyRunning, and leaves the running
  task untouched. OnRequest() drops it before it changes any state, so
  it no longer replaces the running task's update callback or its last
  seen sequence number. Before:
  - ExecuteActions, and Trigger or Continue during an action execution,
    got FAILED while the work kept running, so the server ended a task
    that Chrome kept running.
  - A Trigger during a Glic session had no guard: Chrome started a
    second invocation and replaced the running task's update callback.
- StopActuationRequest no longer resets the action runner or cancels
  the actor task itself. Its STOPPED reply is terminal, so the shared
  path stops the owned work. That cancels the actor task only if this
  handler's trigger created it, so a Stop can't cancel another caller's
  task on a shared Glic instance. A Stop that arrives before that actor
  task exists no longer calls CancelTask().
- A session that a synchronous FAILED or STOPPED ends now records
  ExecutionOutcome kTerminalFailed or kTerminalStopped. Before, these
  sessions either went on, or (for Stop) the destructor recorded
  kDestroyedBeforeCompletion. Dashboards: Stop moves from
  kDestroyedBeforeCompletion to kTerminalStopped.
- Structure: OnRequest() returns early for a duplicate start and passes
  the rest to DispatchRequest(). The synchronous path and
  HandleTerminalUpdate() share EndSession(), so the synchronous path now
  also stops the reconnect grace. The FAILED replies that this CL
  touches use a new Reject() helper. RejectAndEndSession() keeps the
  reply's sequence number and the session end in the handler.
- A handler now serves one task, so ProcessTriggerOrContinueActuationRequest()
  no longer resets the task milestones on a second Trigger, and
  NonContinuationRetriggerOnSameContext_ResetsTaskMilestoneTimings is
  removed. The server sends Continue or Stop after YIELD, never Trigger.

Known gaps:
- A duplicate Continue during a running Glic session is still
  processed, because Chrome cannot tell it from a valid Continue after
  a YIELD.
- A request that is replayed after the task ended reaches a new
  handler and is processed.
- A synchronous FAILED before an actor task exists ends the session,
  but the Glic invocation keeps running, because there is no actor
  task to cancel yet (b:582416328).
Request sequence numbers (b:580609948) would fix the first two.

End-to-end check against the hosted server, with a test-only build that
re-sends a mutated copy of the real Trigger in the middle of a task:
- Duplicate start: no reply; the task goes on to YIELD.
- Missing task metadata, version mismatch, missing payload: FAILED with
  the live handler's next sequence number; the server accepts it as
  final, and both sides end the task.
- No injection: the task completes normally.

Design: https://docs.google.com/document/d/10sir3cbIab_2dHtwJ7J8zx6DNSrh9yTLbe9OeUZbcrw (P10, §6)

[Public Transit] Raise EntryPointSentinelStation timeout to 12s

After crrev.com/c/8714139 added PageHitTestDataCondition to page
Stations, "Page has hit test data" timeouts appeared in 228 CQ runs
(194/2419 builds) and 60 CI runs (52/794 builds) over ~13.5 hours,
raising cold-start test flake rates such as
SidePanelContainerCoordinatorIntegrationTest (0.25% -> 2.28%,
1.16% -> 3.31%) and PaymentRequestPaymentAppUiSkipPreloadTest
(1.02% -> 2.73%).

~78% (221/283) of these failures happen on Trip 1 out of
EntryPointSentinelStation on the first test in a fresh instrumentation
process. ConditionWaiter starts the 5000 ms poll timer before running
the synchronous startMainActivity* trigger, which takes up to 11.1 s on
cold start, and Station.requireToBeInSameTask() replaces
ActivityExistsInNewTaskCondition (10 s) with
ActivityExistsInAnyTaskCondition (default 5 s) when the origin has no
ActivityElement. That leaves only a single post-trigger check before
Viz has aggregated hit test data for the first frame.

Override noopTo(), runTo(), and runOnUiThreadTo() on
EntryPointSentinelStation to default to a 12 s transition timeout while
still allowing callers' explicit withTimeout() calls to override it.

c95b3b585273e865ae614d0f170efd4a148ab442

75acdb1eb39db3eaecc877a985a685576038bfd1 [ diff ] 
Only query the sticky modal for the real default browser modal

DefaultBrowserModalUI queried IsDefaultBrowserModalSticky() for every
page load of chrome://default-browser-modal. When the sticky modal is
enabled, the page binds default_browser_modal.mojom.PageHandlerFactory
in connectedCallback(), but that binder is only registered when the
modal prompt surface is enabled (Windows 11+). Opening the URL directly
(e.g. on Windows 10, or in the WebUI URL browser tests) therefore killed
the renderer with "No binder found". It also enrolled clients in the
sticky modal experiment without showing them the modal.

Only report the page as sticky when it is the real modal dialog
(is_modal=true), which is always set by default_browser::Show() and is
already the condition for the page calling showUI() over Mojo.

Also explicitly disable kDefaultBrowserStickyModal in
DefaultBrowserModalDialogManagerInteractiveTest, which verifies the
non-sticky histograms, so it does not depend on the fieldtrial testing
config.

media: Finish EME visibility ratio browser tests after playback start

EncryptedMediaVisibilityRatioReportTest played a 2.75s encrypted WebM
clip all the way to `ENDED` in all five tests even though
VisibilityRatioAtPlaybackStart is computed on the first clean paint once
the CDM attaches and playback begins. Add a stopAfterPlaybackStart query
option in eme_player.html that pauses and finishes after 'playing' plus
two animation frames so the visibility ratio is recorded without waiting
for full-clip playback.

This cuts local runtime across the five visibility ratio browser tests
from 27.1s (~5.4s/test) to 5.0s (~1.0s/test).

f31f9e06b9064df41e6b2d6454c2ac3b7f513adc [ diff ] 
[Transition] Normalize and latch DismissReason in dialog and sheet coordinators

Normalizes container-specific dismissal causes into @DismissReason in
AdaptiveUiDialogCoordinator and AdaptiveUiSheetCoordinator so the
presenter can report AdaptiveUiObserver#onDismissed(@DismissReason int)
without leaking DialogDismissalCause or StateChangeReason into the entry
API:

- AdaptiveUiDialogCoordinator:
  - Changes onDialogDismissed from Runnable to Callback<Integer>
    receiving the resolved @DismissReason.
  - Latches in-dialog dismiss requests (PRIMARY_ACTION,
    SECONDARY_ACTION, CLOSE_BUTTON, BACK_PRESS) and explicit
    setDismissReason(@DismissReason int) overrides so ambiguous
    container dismissal causes (such as NEGATIVE_BUTTON_CLICKED or
    NAVIGATE_BACK_OR_TOUCH_OUTSIDE on WINDOW-scoped dialogs) preserve
    the exact user interaction that triggered dismissal.
  - Maps between @DismissReason and @DialogDismissalCause.

- AdaptiveUiSheetCoordinator:
  - Adds an onSheetDismissed Callback<Integer> constructor parameter,
    setDismissReason(@DismissReason int), and
    onSheetHidden(@StateChangeReason int), reporting the resolved
    @DismissReason once from destroy().
  - Maps between @DismissReason and @StateChangeReason.

- DismissReason:
  - Documents each constant and removes unused REPLACED.

NO_IFTTT=AdaptiveUi(Dialog/Sheet)Coordinator mapping are updated in this CL. No changes needed for DialogDismissalCause and StateChangeReason.

[iOS][RA] Restore table selection after reloading bottom sheet data

-[UITableView reloadData] clears UIKit's selection while the checkmark
stays on `selectedRow`, so the two no longer match.

what the user sees. This affects the remote actor credential sheet,

ab57da5e0bd121debc6d25f6cd53d934605bf7ad

5b523f6a578a894baacf91bc00305a2a451ae54c [ diff ] 
ContainerTiming: attribute paints and create entries at paint time

Paints were replayed through the attribution tracker at presentation
time, when the DOM and tracker may have changed since the paint, and
entries took the navigation id current at presentation.

Attribute each paint and grow the container records during paint, and
create the entries at the end of paint, like element timing. The
presentation callback only sets their timing and navigation id with
FinalizeEntry(), then reports them. Only the records that grew since
the last entries are kept, so creating entries does not scan every
record.

Changing or removing containertiming, or detaching the element, after
paint no longer affects entries for that paint. See [1] and [2].
Disconnecting a container root still drops its record, but an entry
already created for that paint is reported.

[1] https://github.com/w3c/container-timing/issues/87
[2] https://github.com/w3c/container-timing/pull/88

🌺 Disable PeriodicBackgroundSyncBrowserTest.MultipleEventsFired on Android

The following test failed all retry attempts (hard-failed) in 4/25
recent builds on android-arm64-tests (6/40 builds on android-arm64-tests
plus 10 hard-failed CI runs and 101 flaky CI runs across 14 Android CI
builders in 7d):
  PeriodicBackgroundSyncBrowserTest.MultipleEventsFired (Android only)

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/android-arm64-tests/29054

Why disable rather than revert:
Chronic RunLoop::Run() timeout while waiting for PopConsoleString() in
PeriodicBackgroundSyncBrowserTest.MultipleEventsFired across 14 Android
CI builders with no regression range or plausible culprit commit.

To run/deflake this test on CQ before re-enabling, add the following
footer(s) to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android:android-arm64-tests|content_browsertests

This is mitigation, not a fix. The bug tracks the real investigation and
is assigned to the owning team.

c94728b043f28db26b68252d32dc312bc265c07c

8343d85a4d76fcb049500881720640b260d32c75 [ diff ] 
[update_client] Make structs aggregates; use designated initializers

The Chromium style plugin exempts aggregate types from the
"complex class needs an out-of-line constructor/destructor" checks.
Many structs owned by //components/update_client/OWNERS declared
defaulted special members only to satisfy those checks, which made
them non-aggregates. Remove those declarations, and the memberwise
constructors, so the types become C++20 aggregates. Use designated
initializers at call sites, and inline single-use locals.

Converted types:
- components/update_client: CrxComponent, CrxInstaller::InstallParams,
  CrxUpdateItem, protocol_request::{OS, Updater, UpdateCheck, Data,
  Ping, App, Request}, ProtocolParser::{Data, Operation, Pipeline, App,
  Results}, Unpacker::Result.
- components/component_updater: ComponentInfo, ComponentRegistration
  (previously a 13-argument constructor).
- chrome/updater: AppCompletionInfo, ObserverCompletionInfo,
  MSIDirEntry, MSIHeader, PersistedDataEvent::RegisteredApp,
  tagging::TagArgs, PolicyServiceProxyConfiguration, TagParsingResult,
  InstallerOutcome, ProtocolParserXML::{App, Results},
  TaskScheduler::TaskInfo.
- chrome/browser: UpdaterState::State,
  PKIMetadataComponentInstallerService::{LoadedPKIMetadata,
  MtcCaIdAndLandmarkTrustAnchorIds}, CrOSComponentInstaller::LoadInfo,
  ExtensionUpdater::{CheckParams, InProgressCheck}, UpdateState.
- extensions/browser/updater: ExtensionUpdateData,
  ExtensionUpdateCheckParams.
- components/winhttp: ProxyInfo.

Delete the .cc files that only defined defaulted special members.

Defaults that constructors used to set are now default member
initializers: CrxUpdateItem::state, ExtensionUpdateCheckParams'
priority and install_immediately, and the ComponentRegistration bools,
which mirror CrxComponent. The
supports_group_policy_enable_component_updates bool defaults to false,
which is the value an omitted designator would get.

Side effects, none functional:
- Types that hold a base::Version lose their noexcept moves until
  base::Version's move constructor is noexcept.
- protocol_request::OS becomes copyable, and
  ExtensionUpdater::InProgressCheck becomes movable.
- Special members are now inline, so binary size may change slightly.

tree
9b5ac06651aff1e0f58ae7179139b908e970847a
parent
ee087af933ff2699c004d12ee12dd279f01edb93 [ diff ] 
[viz] Preserve initial ClientSharedImage in SurfaceResourceHolder

When the same ResourceId is submitted across multiple CompositorFrames
over Mojo, TransferableResource deserialization creates a new unowned
ClientSharedImage instance each time.
DisplayResourceProvider::ReceiveFromChild() keeps the initial
ClientSharedImage instance for an already-imported ResourceId and later
records the Display Compositor's release SyncToken on it via
EndDisplayCompositorAccess(). However,
SurfaceResourceHolder::ReceiveFromChild() unconditionally overwrote
ref.shared_image with each newly received instance. With
UseAutomaticSyncTokenManagement enabled,
SurfaceResourceHolder::UnrefResources() ended up calling EndImport() on
a newer ClientSharedImage instance instead of the one updated by
DisplayResourceProvider, dropping the release SyncToken.

To fix this, this CL only assigns ref.shared_image in
SurfaceResourceHolder::ReceiveFromChild() when refs_received_from_child
is 1, ensuring SurfaceResourceHolder retains the initial
ClientSharedImage instance.

Bug: 40286368, 579891738, 575052822

Abort DiceSignedInProfileCreator if initiator account is removed
📋 Metadata

ee087af933ff2699c004d12ee12dd279f01edb93
[ log ]

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>

tree
abdca676d4ab7693cced9f3573db6a5a94fee4d6
parent
d613bed8386984fe490c6cd8aff7002c512748a6 [ diff ] 
Abort DiceSignedInProfileCreator if initiator account is removed

If the initiator account is removed from the source profile while the
target profile and its refresh tokens are loading asynchronously,
DiceSignedInProfileCreator::OnNewProfileTokensLoaded() calls
AccountsMutator::MoveAccount() for an account that is no longer in the
source profile's AccountTrackerService. This triggers a CHECK failure in
AccountTrackerService::PickAccountIdForAccount() when seeding an empty
AccountInfo in the target profile.

Check that the initiator account still has a refresh token in the source
profile before moving accounts in OnNewProfileTokensLoaded(). When the
initiator account is missing, abort the profile creation flow by
invoking the completion callback with nullptr, and either schedule the
newly created profile for deletion or clear the initial browser-window
keep-alive on an existing loaded profile.

> > Disabled text fields and checkboxes painted an opaque dark gray fill in
> > dark mode so the page behind them never showed through, while light mode
> > uses a translucent fill over a lighten layer; mirror the light-mode
> > alphas in dark mode behind a kill-switch feature.
> >
> > PaintButton draws through PaintLightenLayer, so the alpha change also
> > affects the anti-aliased edges of disabled buttons.
> >

tree
63c8047ef52911ca22e2ac88fb13ac92a9f78789
parent
7936815b38d22861cf3951e5c758408d582ab9f8 [ diff ] 
[AdTracker] Record actual script URL rather than CNAME alias URL

When a script response matches a SubresourceFilter rule via a DNS CNAME
alias (behind kSendCnameAliasesToSubresourceFilterFromRenderer),
BaseFetchContext::CalculateResourceAnnotations evaluates the alias URL
against the filterlist. However, FrameFetchContext was also passing the
alias URL to AdTracker::CalculateIfAdSubresource, causing AdTracker to
record the CNAME alias URL in context_known_ad_scripts_ rather than the
script's actual request URL.

Because V8 compiles and executes scripts using their request/response
URL rather than DNS CNAME aliases, AdTracker::OnScriptRegistered failed
to match CNAME-cloaked ad scripts at execution time.

Pass resource_request.Url() to AdTracker::CalculateIfAdSubresource so
that CNAME-tagged scripts are properly tracked on execution, and add
SimRequest DNS alias support and end-to-end AdTrackerSimTest coverage.

Skip mass timing out prefetch web tests under linux msan

Prefetch tests and their virtual test suites are experiencing so many
timeouts under msan, that they're causing the entire
linux-blink-msan-rel bot to time out. We skip all of the virtual test
suites in this configuration, but preserve coverage of the non-virtual
tests by marking them as flaky timeouts.

77feb4e5f55dee3663ff46e773e0e63cdf5dede1 [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Apply pin state updates from the provider

In provider mode, a tab's pin state and action button now update from
the provider's PIN_STATE update rather than TabListLayoutDelegate's tab
observer.

TabListLayoutDelegate#onTabPinnedStateChanged is still called in
provider mode, so it now returns early. Otherwise a single pin would
move the card twice: once from the observer and once from the provider's
move event.

When groups are supported, the card is detached while its pin state
changes and re-added at the same index, as onTabPinnedStateChanged does
without a provider. The vertical tab strip uses a different row type for
pinned tabs, and this keeps the adapter from seeing that change in
place.

Bug: 562590772

4f4830d6a755b580cebd36283c37b83035c48dec [ diff ] 
[WebAudio] Render audiobuffersource-multi-channels.html at 8820 Hz

The test compares 6 x 44100 samples one by one through
assert_array_approx_equals, which takes ~34 s on linux-blink-msan-rel
against a 50 s budget and times out on the first attempt in most runs.

Render at 8820 Hz (44100 / 5) so the 1 s 5.1 tone compares 5x fewer
samples, and regenerate the reference WAV at that rate: the same sines
createToneBuffer() produces, rounded to int16 (x32767 for positive and
x32768 for negative values). Widen the tolerance from 1 to 2 LSB so
decoders that scale symmetrically by 1/32768 also pass.

tree
9831c30fe12449ea9431a7745eeed53c83596817
parent
cb5d98a5d95df6fccf7be2550b01c66df7b64c06 [ diff ] 
webui: Bind navigation WebUIURLLoaderFactory on navigation task runner

This CL adds an optional receiver task runner parameter to
SelfDeletingURLLoaderFactory and
CreateWebUIURLLoaderFactoryWithoutOriginLock, and binds the navigation
WebUIURLLoaderFactory receiver onto the navigation network response
task runner behind features::kPrioritizeWebUINavigationURLLoader.

During browser startup, browser-initiated WebUI navigations create an
in-process WebUIURLLoaderFactory whose Mojo receiver previously bound
to the default UI thread task queue. When the UI thread is busy with
startup tasks, the CreateLoaderAndStart call for the navigation waited
behind normal UI tasks before starting the WebUI resource load, even
though the response client is bound to the high-priority navigation
network response task runner.

On Linux Release Crossbench browser-startup (N=32), this improves
Startup.FirstWebContents.NonEmptyPaint3 P50 (median) by -16.5 ms (-3.3%,
95% CI [-32.5, -6.0] ms) on google_startup.

[WebUI] Stop importing gated cr_components in Android desktop tests

History, NTP and Composebox mocha tests imported helpers from
chrome://resources/cr_components/{history,searchbox} directly. Those
tests also run in android_browsertests on Android desktop, which blocks
dropping the duplicate cr_components/history and searchbox grdps from
optimized Android desktop builds where the pages bundle them.

- Re-export the history.mojom symbols from chrome://history/history.js
  and import them from the page barrel in History tests.
- Define the searchbox test factories in searchbox_test_utils.ts so NTP
  and Composebox tests load them from chrome://webui-test instead of
  cr_components/searchbox.

5c4d1c36cb398a917d1f97dfc6a32aa7c2a4a850 [ diff ] 
[MiraclePtr] Use raw_ptr in base::flat_set fields

Rewrites the remaining `base::flat_set<T*>` fields that the
`chromium-containers` clang plugin check flags once `raw_ptr` is
enforced in `base::internal::flat_tree` (crrev.com/c/8293439). This
aligns with the broader MiraclePtr safety initiative to protect against
use-after-free vulnerabilities.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-rewritten
instances.

Bug: b:501142609
Doc: go/miracleptr-in-containers

Remove obsolete PageActionType variants from page histograms

Remove the 10 PageActionType variants in page/histograms.xml whose
corresponding PageActionIconType enum values are deprecated and no
longer registered in kPageActionProperties:

- ClickToCall
- LocalCardMigration
- PriceTracking
- QRCodeGenerator
- ReaderMode
- SaveIban
- SendTabToSelf
- SharingHub
- SideSearch
- SMSRemoteFetcher

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Variants for deprecated PageActionIconType entries removed from PageActionController histograms.
